ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e5422	Mr President. Good morning, Mrs. Minister! They obviously want to take the Federal Competition Authority to the short line by amending the Competition Act and restrict it in its action. Now you are calling for an EU directive. However, this EU Directive only provides for a regular report to be published. However, you want an overwhelming report, a reporting obligation, so that you also have the opportunity to get information in ongoing procedures, for example, and on policy it would be possible to intervene with this important authority. In recent years, the competition authority has often shown cartel languages – most recently in the construction sector. So it seems that politics wants to take this independent authority to the line. That is why my question is: 92M ‘Why Dangers Do you propose the independence of the Federal Competition Authority and the protection necessary for the performance of its tasks against political influence by proposing a superfluous supervisory right over the Federal Competition Authority by amending the Competition Act?"
ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e5455	Mrs Bundesminister, please.
ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e5471	Yes, your question is, I think, important. The answer is quite clear: that you do not need to have any fears, and that the premise that is somehow within the question does not apply, because the independence of the Federal Competition Authority is in no way restricted by an information possibility in any form. The Federal Constitution – I not only rely on the ECN Directive, which is now to be implemented, but I refer to the Federal Constitution – requires an appropriate information right. This is a right of information and not an obligation to provide information to independent authorities. This is to be implemented in a simple law, and that is what we are doing now. I think it's very important to distinguish: We have a right of information from the ministry, and that is not a right of supervision; it is not equal to that and is therefore not overwhelming from my point of view. The review has given many inputs. My intention has always been – and we had so anchored it in the proposal, but we will make it even clearer now – that we will also adopt the OGH's proposals in order to say that quite clearly. We have taken over this comment and we have also made additional clarifications. In what way? – Home searches are explicitly excluded – mentioned in the law – and there is no right of information in this case; that is particularly important to me. The independence of the Federal Competition Authority – which is about independence in execution – is guaranteed. However, there is an information right for each authority in Austria, for financial market supervision as well as for E-Control, as for the RTR. That's where we call for the Constitution: Information is not like guidance.

There will also be a framework training premium for those who report for the framework training: four times 200 euros. It is very important to me to point out the following: There is a paradigm change, because in the future, assistance missions of military soldiers – and not the basic military servants – are to be performed. On the one hand, this raises the importance of the militia, on the other hand it attracts and also improves the ground military service, because more time remains for military training – and it is the main task of the ground defense service to ensure military training for possible use. The basic military service is to become a staff pool for the military. This not only strengthens the militia, but also attracts the ground defense service, because both training is carried out for use and a civil benefit is made, as certain elements can also be used in civil life. On the current occasion, I would also like to take a short look at the change in the business classification of the Federal Ministry of Defence, which is being launched in these days. This is a reform of the central office. It is not a military reform, nor is it a military profile – the structure of the force remains unchanged, the locations remain untouched – but it is a very important and important innovation: the triggering of the military leadership from the Federal Ministry of Defence – a separation between the administration that continues to take place in the Federal Ministry of State Defense, and the military leadership that will be taken over by the Directorate-General for State Defense in the future. This strengthens the force because it shortens administrative paths and thus allows for faster decisions and faster action. There are clear structures and clear responsibilities, and this is especially important for an intervention organization such as the Austrian Federal Army. [[Congratulations on MEPs of the ÖVP.]] Mrs. Federal Minister, I congratulate the implementation of this structural reform of the central office, because it strengthens the capacity for use, the willingness to use the Austrian Federal Government and thus serves the security of Austria and the security of our population within the meaning of the Republic of Austria.

ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e9280	Madam President. Dear Federal Ministers! Values Advisors! Ladies and gentlemen! High house! It is therefore very unfortunate that the government parties did not agree to the request for the restructuring of the Allentsteig area as a security island in the committee. This would have been a first possible step towards an autarchic barracks. Especially the abolition of the kitchen of troops had many negative effects. The food is currently centrally cooked in Wiener Neustadt, packed, frozen and then transported by truck to Allensteig. There they only need to be warmed up. Apart from the fact that the quality probably does not come to that of a military kitchen, of course there is also a great burden on the environment. Transport routes have to be taken into account and it is particularly detrimental that a lot of packaging waste is caused by the individual packaging. In addition, there is no regional purchase of food. Especially in the forest district there are no large food customers, so it would be really important that the barracks also buy their food from the regional producers and dealers. [[Congratulations with the SPÖ.]] For the soldiers, it is a disadvantage that a flexible preparation of the food in the barracks, for example, if they come back from the night use, is not possible because there are only these ready dishes and no food on site to cook something. A change to the kitchen of troops directly in the barracks would be very welcome only for quality or environmental reasons and would also be an important step towards Autarkie. Another requirement of the application is the restructuring of the Allentsteig area of the troops to a security island. Safety Islands are barracks which are independent in each area, which provide independent water and energy, but also have food. From the point of view of the population, security islands are very important anchor points in the case of a blackout. As early as 2020, the security policy review warned of a threatening blackout. It's not the question of whether a blackout comes, but when a blackout comes. Austria has passed it several times, among other things already in this year. It is therefore necessary to make as many barracks as possible in Austria a security island. With a joint request, you, Mrs Bundesminister, will now be commissioned to draw up an overall concept, including budgeting, for autonomous barracks throughout Austria, and to present it to Parliament by the end of the year. The ball is now with you and your ministry. I call for rapid implementation. Thank you.

I don't know if You have already asked yourself why, in some countries, there are ten candidates for teaching and not with us. It's an exciting question, I think, to find out. They are expanding the Elementary Education Colleges – as it now looks, but cannot be occupied all the places because there are too few interested parties. So I think we need a discussion about the educational profession of the future. What is this supposed to include? What can he do? This also includes considering this educational profession as a life-spent profession, that cross-border entry is possible, that changeover and re-entry are easier and that there are also opportunities for ascent or ascent. A new educational profession also includes a new feedback, a new leadership and a new training culture in this field, which promotes and also rewards commitment. We NEOS believe that the teacher profession, whether for school or kindergarten, is the most important among all professions. For working with our children, we need the best – the most motivated and the best – and these best need the best working conditions. In this large area, we need a discussion and not: It's all right anyway, and we're going as far as we've done!

ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e14764	Mr Josef Smolle has now come to speak. Please, honourable Member.
ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e14789	Mr President. Mr. Federal Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! My Dear Ones Ladies and gentlemen! From this broad range of requests, which are being discussed on this item on the agenda today, I would like to draw up a point that has been raised briefly, namely the question of complementary medicine. The basic question that arises is how to say very responsible colleagues from the medical field, these are valuable medical measures that bring something, and other, probably equally responsible Members think, that is ineffective. To this end, we must: It is necessary to keep in mind once, which leads to a disease being cured – these are three components that can come together. The first component is the so-called objective efficacy, which you find in clinical studies: that a drug really works. The second component, which we already have quite a bit in mind, is the so-called placebo effect. In reality, this is the millennium-old healing effect of the doctor-patient contact. The third component, which is probably the most important – and which is most rarely thought of – is the fact that nature makes a lot of itself right. I give you a fictitious example: We all have the Covid-19 disease in mind and know that it is pretty harmless for 80 percent of those affected. Now let us imagine that we treat all these patients with an objectively ineffective drug. 80 percent will say afterwards: That helped me, that went well – even if it was completely ineffective. This explains that it is really possible to trigger enthusiasm with ineffective measures over years, sometimes over centuries. We are now in the position: Methods which are verifiably effective belong to the relevant subjects in the general education and belong to the rule provision. In the case of measures where the proof of effectiveness fails or does not exist, there is no need for a chair. That is why we say that research should be carried out, that should be further investigated, but at present there is no right to anchor the university. I would like to conclude with two positive aspects. The first positive aspect is that in recent years complementary medicine has become increasingly aware of this challenge, is also challenging and brings studies on track – that is positive. Second: If You remember that I spoke of these three components, which ultimately contribute to healing: I can say that from the beginning of the 2000s onwards, the Austrian universities have been telling their medical students that we need a biopsychosocial understanding of health and illness. Bio means that medicine is, in part, a natural science that has made enormous progress and is still bringing it. To some extent, however, medicine is also characterized by a psychosocial dimension, and it is equally important. - Thank you.

ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e17309	Mr President. Federal Minister! I am now at the speaker's desk for the fourth time to try to prevent animal pain that is happening in Austria – the fourth time, because it is necessary to make a deadline, because animal welfare applications do not come back to the agenda of a meeting of the Health Committee or because the next meeting of the Health Committee will only take place in autumn, as we have heard. One of the proposals for which we want to set a deadline concerns the prohibition of the position of pigs on whole-splitted soils. Ladies and gentlemen, about 60 percent of pigs in Austria are kept on so-called full-column floors. This is according to point 2.2.2 of Appendix 5 of the 1. Animal husbandry regulation allowed. The floor is consistently perforated, which means it has continuous columns and is usually made of concrete. The masts are beams with a width of 1.8 centimetres, and the distance between the beams, that is, the gap, is 8 centimetres. That is, in clear text, the pigs are moving permanently on these columns. When you know the feet of the pigs, you know that this is extremely harmful for these and also for the joints. A measles weighing up to 85 kilograms must have only at least 0.55 square metres of surface area. Ladies and gentlemen, what does that mean on these full-column floors? – 0.55 square meters means 55 centimetres times 1 meter. With a pig with 85 kilos, you can imagine – and this minimum area is usually not exceeded – what the need these pigs actually have in these stables and how it is when the pigs have to make their decoding there at the same time. This decoupling should be made easier for farmers through these whole-column floors. Pigs are highly social beings that we can really equate with people. I think none of us wants to live in his pus or urine or above, because the whole stench rises and dominates ammonia tank, which causes inflammation of the eyes in the pigs. I got a picture here. [[The speaker puts a table on the speaker's desk, on which house pigs held on a full - split floor can be seen.]] So, ladies and gentlemen, it looks everywhere. This is not an individual case of farmed for fattening, but so it is simply in Austria. But what do the media always show us? – There is the yes! natural piggy, the dear piglets running around with his farmer and is funny and clean on the meadow and pasture. There's no such thing in Austria! Then there is the AMA advertising, which shows how pigs enter just on a blitz-clean full-column floor. I think there's never been a pig on that before. It was originally written in English and, as a minimum, called for a lying area that is “physically comfortable”. This formulation, however, was completely falsely translated into German by “largely appropriate”. The EU did so after years of this year. February 2016 corrects because it has recognized that this has been mistranslated. It is now stated in the official German version of this directive that pigs must be provided with a “physically pleasant” lying area. This is the case in the EU Regulation. The original formulation “adequate in size” was replaced by “sizedly pleasant” instead of “physically pleasant”. What would it mean to replace this formulation with “physically agreeable ”? – 92 percent of all pigs have painful joints on the whole column floor and experience injuries. This is not “physically pleasing”, so the full-column floors would have to be changed as a result of this EU regulation – if properly implemented. One more thing, ladies and gentlemen: The VGT, the club against animal factories, has run a petition against these full-column floors. These have already signed nearly 84 000 people. 84,000 people have signed a petition that is not so familiar. In the foreseeable future, the centennial limit will be exceeded. That is to say, it is a concern of the Austrians to do something. I really call on everyone – including every Member – to support this petition so that we can eliminate this animal suffering in Austria so that we can finally say: Yes, we in Austria are again pioneers in animal welfare, yes, we are looking at our animals, and yes, we really want there to be animal welfare in agriculture, rather than that it is, as Mr Farm Chamber President Moosbrugger said, that farmers' well-being seems to be ahead of animal welfare! [[Congratulations with the SPÖ.]] We want both to feel comfortable: the animals feel comfortable and the farmers feel comfortable. There, Minister Köstinger has money to take over and see that this animal welfare can really take place in the farms in Austria, ladies and gentlemen! But it's not just about these full-column floors. A number of deadlines for applications will be put to the vote later, but they will not be debated. This is about the intoxication-free piglets that we have also treated. These are still available in Austria. It is constantly being discussed, and excuses are constantly being found why the piglets cannot be stunned, why they cannot be castrated painlessly. I think it's possible to do that. There is is an isofluran, an anesthetic that in Germany even the farmer is allowed to use after a certificate of expertise which he has to provide. In other words, a piglets castration with anaesthesia would also be in Austria with 1. January 2022 possible if we allowed it. That'd be quick. We still have the chick savior and the chick killing. That too would no longer have to be, even there are already measures, ladies and gentlemen, to prevent the whole. We want minimum penalties on animal transport, because there is still too much going on. It's not about animal transport when the farmer drives his bull to the cow or anything else, we don't mean it. We mean mass animal transport that takes place on the road. There must finally be minimum penalties to show them: It is no longer possible to deal with the animals in Austria, ladies and gentlemen! [[Congratulations with the SPÖ and representatives of the FPÖ.]] I really think it's high time to do something. That is why we have tabled this deadline request. We want the Health Committee to be still up to 1. The issue of animals and animal welfare in Austria was discussed in July. We want this Health Committee to finally take action to the benefit of animals that something goes on here. There are many things we could do. As an example – because it was back in the media today – I take the illegal puppies trade. What could we do without anyone seeing it? – There's no money from the state, none of us hit, we just have to say: Puppies or dogs imported into Austria must have at least the rabies vaccination. A rabie vaccination may take place before the 15th century. Don't give the dog a week of life because you can't give it there. That means that the dogs would be 15 weeks old, no more puppies and therefore uninteresting for these dog mafia. That is how we get away from this illegal puppie trade, ladies and gentlemen. We could do many things if the ÖVP is ready to do something for the benefit of the animals in Austria.

ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e19067	Mr President. Mr. Federal Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! Ladies and gentlemen! [[Abg. Belakovich: Don't promise too much, the Federal Chancellor...!]] Even now, I believe that certain measures that we are still maintaining are necessary so that we can look well into the future. [[Congratulations with the ÖVP and MEPs of the Greens.]] But I come now to speak to the Medical Products Act. This is primarily a technical one, because three old EU directives have been replaced by two new EU regulations. Therefore, it was also necessary to revise the Austrian law matter thoroughly. I would essentially like to limit myself to two aspects of this conflict of law. The one concerns research and development – how do you get new medical products? – and the second is: How to achieve as much patient and patient safety as possible in the area where medical devices are used? Medical product is a very broad concept. At EU level, around 500 000 different medical devices are in circulation and on the market. This extends from the highly complicated stepmaker on one side to the patch that you stick to a wound, on the other side. The law is now designed in such a way that there are very strict specifications so that newly developed medical devices can be tested even once on patients. This is largely based on analogy to drug testing, which is now uniformly regulated across Europe, and this is very, very much in the interests of patient and patient safety. The second is that there are, of course, some feasibility studies in advance of development, which take place in vitro, i.e. have nothing directly to do with a patient and have no effect on their treatment or diagnostics. For these feasibility studies, there are, as it were, lower requirements, which need to be reported and sought. But what seems particularly important to me is: For both types of studies, both those that are direct to the patient and those in advance, there needs to be an ethic vote, also in analogy to the medicines. As far as the application to patients is concerned, there are the so-called active implantable medical devices, the standard product is the heart pacemaker. There are these personal records, i.e. as soon as a problem occurs somewhere in a product, the patients who have this pacemaker can be informed by name. This is an important security step, of course always with data protection, but that is correct. I would like to thank my colleague, Mr Heinisch-Hosek, for considering the fact that other products, which are not now covered by the actively implantable products, are also covered. One must certainly look further and, if necessary, extend this registration requirement to other products – in turn data protection compliant but primarily in the interests of safety. [[It was in favour of the ÖVP as well as of representatives of Greens and SPÖ.]] I think it's a very good law package that's available here. It is probably considered, it brings European unity and it brings high standards of security for our country. I ask for broad consent. - Thank you.

The government template deals with the EU legal requirements of EU Regulation 2019/1381, which essentially includes two points: on the one hand, the use of the standard data format for the transmission of information in the application for approval, on the other hand, the definition of which information may not be treated confidentially, and now there is a final list of the information for which the authority may allow confidential treatment, provided that it is requested by the applicant. A much more exciting topic, however, is how we will deal with so-called new genetic engineering in the future. We already know that, even in conventional genetic engineering, great news has been announced to us: We will feed Africa, we will save pesticides and we will increase the yield of hectares. None of this has happened, on the contrary: The use of pesticides in the cultivation areas of genetically modified plants has partly increased by threefold, some of the yields have even fallen, and Africa is still starved. The incomes fell at least for farmers, only the revenues for Monsanto have increased. Austria has played a pioneering role in the question of antigen technology and can do so in the new genetic engineering. There are the same arguments in the new genetic engineering. Climate change has been added to it, because of which the dry tolerance has been taken in, i.e. the gene shears are intended to breed plants that are dry-tolerant. The truth is: There are just 120 plants in development, and these 120 plants are not about dryness tolerance, but about a rape that is herbicid-resistant – we already know – it's about a longer-lasting salad and it's about potatoes without black spots. ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e20567	Mr President. Mr. Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! Some of my previous speakers have already mentioned this: This block deals in particular with the extension or reform of short-term work, which is being decided today. Why? – It's obvious: Fortunately, thanks to openings, thanks to vaccinations, the Austrian economy is sustainable, unemployment is falling and employment is rising. That is to say, it will simply be no longer necessary to implement appropriate measures, appropriate support measures, appropriate aid measures to overcome the corona crisis, as they have been set up so far. However – and precisely that is why we need these two models, which may perhaps be confusing for some people – there are still certain sectors affected by partial lockdowns, partial closures, of measures that cannot yet rise as much as we would like, where it makes sense to preserve these structures, where, of course, – and Mr Loacker will be able to address the danger of structural conservation of short work afterwards – a healthy level, a healthy relationship must be found with regard to the question: Do we still keep these plants over water for a longer time, or do we possibly prevent the necessary structural change that exists in the economy? I think we have found very good compromises. One thing is: By the end of this year, the corona-short work, as we know it so far, is still under way for those sectors which are still particularly affected by the crisis which are still suffering from corona measures. However, on the one hand, they must prove that their sales are more than 50 percent compared to the previous year, and on the other hand that the measures are also taking place. On the other hand, we have a short-term work model, which, in principle, is very closely linked to the corona model, when it comes to the question of net replacement rates, i.e. the replacement of wages, which, at the same time, is also clearly trying to prevent it from Takeover effects and abuse effects are present. It is therefore a matter of ensuring that real short-term work is taken up by those enterprises which are in a short-term economic, economic emergency, but which do not do so in order to create liquidity for a later period, but are thus actually supported in the crisis. This is why the reduction of working time to 50 per cent, the retention and also the use of holidays, which must be agreed by common accord between the social partners. Fortunately, this model runs in all these points until the end of June 2022, and then this model is to be evaluated, because what may be an idea: Perhaps this model will be adopted as a standard model for future short work. Then: There has always been short work, and why not take over a model that has proved itself? In this sense, I would ask you to agree to these two requests. I think we will find a broad majority. It has not yet been so far in the Committee, but due to the changes, this should be the case this time. – Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e20790	The amendment is sufficiently supported, properly introduced and is therefore under negotiation. The Federal Minister to whom I am entitled to speak has been reported. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e20815	Mr President. Ladies and gentlemen! High house! Fortunately, we see – it was also mentioned in the speeches – an improvement both in the epidemiological situation and in the labour market. The situation is better at the moment than we expected two, three months ago – fortunately. Vaccination is progressing, and the labour market is recovering in areas where there are no restrictions. This does not mean, however, that the labour market is completely normalised. We have set two priorities in the Ministry of Labour during this pandemic: first of all, the protection of jobs, especially through short-time work – and there I am immediately talking about the new variant and the new models – and secondly, of course, also the protection of workers from illness, from Covid at work. The corona short work – which has already been mentioned – has ensured more than 1 million jobs in a sustainable way, and it has contributed to securing income. This also helps us now in the upturn through the additional consumption impulse that comes from this income. However, this is of course also said that short-time work is an instrument for the crisis, for the failure of demand. Now we have another situation where it goes up. It is therefore necessary to get out of this generous form of short-time work by means of a transitional model. That's exactly what we did in this model. On the one hand, it is about supporting the dynamics of the labour market and, on the other hand, of course, continuing to support the companies that are still particularly affected. Together with the social partners, with the Ministry of Finance, I thank everyone for the constructive way of dialogue, we have developed a model or two models that pursue this very objective. This was not easy because it is not easy to find this balance between dynamic and security, especially in a situation where there is uncertainty as to how the situation will develop in autumn and winter. It is therefore also very important to me that the short-term work phase 5 is a transitional phase, but it is also very unlikely that there will be setbacks in the fight against pandemics. The two variants presented very briefly: Variant 1 is a transition model with reduced funding height. In this model, we have created a transition to a short work that will continue to exist. We also had a short work before the corona pandemic, which was introduced in the financial crisis, and this transitional model is very close to pre-crisis short work. [[President Bures shall take over the chairmanship.]] There is a 15 percent discount from the previous level of aid. However, the net replacement rate for employees remains the same. The minimum working time is usually 50 percent, and the model is available until mid-2022. The social partners and the AMS must be consulted in consultation prior to use, and no holding may normally claim such short work for more than 24 months. The transitional model provides stronger incentives to return to normal employment. The second model is the model of corona short work for massively affected businesses that have to show a turnover loss of at least 50 percent or which are affected by administrative closures. In principle, the minimum working time is 30 percent, and in exceptional cases it is also less possible. This short-term work will be limited until the end of this year in order to give these companies the chance of transition and security for the next six months. I think it's a good compromise formula. As I said, The situation is not easy to assess. The labour market is developing very rapidly – fortunately also very positively – but with these two models in this transition, I think, we have created the right condition. I said that the second important priority of the Ministry was the protection of workers, particularly those who are particularly vulnerable to the pandemic. Pregnants are such a group. We have therefore extended the right of exemption for pregnant women in occupations close to the body until the end of September, provided that the persons concerned have not yet been fully vaccinated. Of course, this is a transitional phase. The reason is relatively simple: These women are severely affected because they work in professions where there is physical contact and distance cannot be observed: Hairdressers, physiotherapists, elementary pedagogues. They would now have stood without this protection from the end of June. Nevertheless, it would have been impossible to immunise everyone because the National Vaccination Body the recommendation on vaccination was issued only a few weeks ago. That is, there are still many who have not yet been vaccinated or have been vaccinated once and do not yet have the full immunization. The more important it is to ensure the safety and health of mothers and children in this transition phase. That's what happens with this request. The costs resulting from this will be completely replaced by the federal government. In other words, the farms are also exempt from these costs. The extension is expected to take place by 1. In July, it was fully linked to the previous regulation. I think this works well and we have time until September to also get the vaccinations for pregnant women on the way. Thank you.

There will be a new look in the future, the photos will also be improved to give more security. So, I say, there are a total of four advantages: The passports and ID cards become more falsifying. The participation of children is absent. Fortunately, the costs remain the same, they will not be increased. And, of course, the passport will also be used in the fight against crime in the future. From birth until the second year of life, children receive a passport free of charge, from two to twelve years are charged 30 euros and from 12 years 75.90 euros as before. So, in summary, it's to say: There is unanimous agreement on this passport novelle. It is about the fight against crime, the criminal prosecution of the money laundering agency, and the exchange of information, even when it comes to the pursuit of terrorist financing. There is also an adaptation to international standards and EU standards. Thank you, Mr Federal Minister, and thank you for your broad support. Thank you.

ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e23259	Madam President. Ladies and gentlemen! Mr. Federal Minister! My previous speaker has already said it in large parts: The main issue of the amendment of the passport law is that we increase the safety criteria of travel documents in Austria. Especially with regard to the identity card, where we know that it is becoming increasingly popular, is becoming increasingly popular, and is being taken more and more, there is now a need to adapt to the need to ensure that the EU regulations and the European level are reached accordingly. As I said, this is about these two fingerprints and the photo. So we simply achieve this by increasing the safety criteria and implementing the European directive on safety criteria, as they do today. The last major change regarding passport, I believe, has been made in 2006. There it is now, I think, already at the time that we are making the appropriate adjustments here too. That is one part: that we pay attention to and that it is implemented that the security of counterfeiting is increased. This happens at the passport also on the side where the photo appears, which is another material, so that a falsification of the photo is no longer as easy as possible. So we make it very difficult for passersby to make the falsification quite clear. On the other hand, this change in the law also improves the exchange of data. This is an improved exchange of data and exchange of information in areas where it is important to be able to pursue, not only in the international context, but also in essence, terrorist financing channels, but also to record other crimes. This means of course we get to a subject area that is already interesting: One is always the possibility of data exchange, the exchange of information, also the knowledge of the information that is then available. There we have seen where we actually still have flaws in our system. It was unfortunately in the wake of the terrorist attack of the Second World War. In November, it was shown that the data were partially available, but the authorities could not carry out the data exchange and the use of information accordingly. The Zerbes Commission, which has been used, has made this clear to us and revealed it to us. We must therefore look at the fact that on the one hand we not only collect the data that has information but also use it. This requires more than the possibility of collecting data, since it needs appropriate structures. That is why we so strongly advocate that we consider modernising the security architecture of our country: that there is a security coordinator, which, in addition to a general government situation centre, networks and structures the intelligence services and the sources of information accordingly. And what it takes quite urgently to counter existing and future threats – we know that terrorism is a very important part – is a counter-terrorism centre. [[Congratulations with the SPÖ.]] This is one of the very essential points that we should implement. I can only appeal to the Minister that we follow European examples here. In Germany this has already been very, very good successes. There should be a platform where experts can exchange and use this information exchange in such a way that we have a good and safe defence against terrorism. ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e27201	Finally, we're coming to 40. Agenda item. We are going into the debate. The applicant shall first speak. Please, Mr Silvan.
ParlaMint-AT_2021-06-17-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00113_d8e27228	Mr President. High house! I have already been asked why we want to amend the federal constitution. That is what we want with this proposal, of course, but it is not as dramatic as it sounds. In fact, it is a question of expanding competence in the field of the extension of the public prosecutor's test competence. Yesterday, too much later on, we discussed the reports of the People's Procuratorate very intensively and agreed – all political groups – that the public prosecutor's work is very good and very valuable, and that this work leads to permanent improvement in the administration, and also that the People's Procuratorate has a very strong voice in the protection of human rights in old and nursing homes, in facilities for children's and youth assistance, and also in institutions for people with disabilities. We know that it is not just recently that federal government, countries, municipalities and cities are outsourced to companies, which sometimes leads to paradoxical situations. The legal form is as follows: The People's Procuratorate may, for example, examine the national hospitals in a federal state, not in another federal state, and it must not examine, for example, the Asfinag and the Federal Forests. That is why we call for an extension of the public prosecutor's audit competence, analogous to the audit competence of the Court of Auditors. This would be an important contribution to transparency and proximity and an important contribution to strengthening the public prosecutor's office, as foreseen in the government programme. I am seeking broad support from this request. Thank you very much. I too wish our national team all the best today. - Thank you.

First of all, I have a question as to what everything should fall within the competence of the People's Procuratorate and where exactly the boundaries are drawn. The application concerns the extension of competence in line with the audit competence of the Court of Auditors. In an analogy to the Court of Auditors, this would also mean that the public prosecutorship is now also responsible for all companies which are at least 25 percent state-owned or in fact state-owned. Also the wording For me, general interest is still a little smeary in this regard. It might be more useful to focus on distributed legal entities in the field of general interest or critical infrastructure, or on companies and legal entities responsible for the performance of state services, which are 100% state-owned. This would therefore include in particular the hospitals, the Vienna lines or, for example, the Asfinag.
